{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1790320905505,"sessionID":"ses_f288f2d8bffeLx1mHfISbknYoh","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"bash","callID":"chatcmpl-tool-11bd21f3c57b4ac58f89cb92d4ac89e6","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"command":"cat /workspace/contracts/runtime.md && echo \"===INFRA===\" && cat /workspace/contracts/infrastructure.md","description":"Read runtime and infrastructure contracts"},"output":"# Runtime\n\nThis file defines the required runtime behavior, service connections and\nenvironment-specific GCP behavior for MediaPulse Ingest. Resource\ntopology, ports, network attachments, IAM and encryption are defined in\n`infrastructure.md` and its files under `services/`.\n\n## Supplied Images\n\n| Component | Provided image tag | Runtime entrypoint |\n|---|---|---|\n| API | `mediapulse/api:1.0.0` | `/app/api` |\n| Projector | `mediapulse/projector:1.0.0` | `/app/projector` |\n| Outbox relay | `mediapulse/relay:1.0.0` | `/app/relay` |\n| Audit archiver | `mediapulse/archiver:1.0.0` | `/app/archiver` |\n\nThe tags are provided local application images. Exact image URIs are provided as the flat `*_image` fields in `/workspace/config/config.json`.\n\n## Configuration\n\nRead the actual runtime values from `/workspace/config/config.json`. It is a\nflat, snake_case JSON object.\n\n| Field | Meaning and required use |\n|---|---|\n| `resource_prefix` | Prefix assigned to this deployment. Use this exact value in the name or tags of every managed resource. |\n| `region` | GCP region. |\n| `gcp_project_id` | GCP Project ID. |\n| `gcp_endpoint_url` | GCP API endpoint. |\n| `api_image` | Exact image reference for the Cloud Run API. |\n| `projector_image` | Exact image reference for the projector Cloud Function. |\n| `relay_image` | Exact image reference for the outbox relay Cloud Function. |\n| `archiver_image` | Exact image reference for the audit archiver Cloud Function. |\n| `db_name` | PostgreSQL database name used by the application. |\n| `db_username` | PostgreSQL username used by the application. |\n| `db_password` | PostgreSQL password used by the application. Treat it as a secret and never emit it in logs or `manifest.json`. |\n\n## API\n\nThe API runs on Cloud Run (v2).\n\nRequired variables:\n- `DATABASE_URL`: `postgresql://<user>:<password>@<host>:5432/<database>?sslmode=disable`\n- `DATASTORE_NAMESPACE`: Cloud Datastore namespace for caching.\n- `FIREBASE_AUTH_JWKS_URL`: URL to fetch Identity Platform JWKS.\n- `GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT`: Exact `gcp_project_id` value from `config.json`.\n\n## Projector Worker\n\nTriggered by Cloud Pub/Sub Push Subscription.\n\nRequired variables:\n- `FIRESTORE_DATABASE`: Cloud Firestore database identifier.\n- `DATASTORE_NAMESPACE`: Cloud Datastore namespace for caching.\n- `GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT`: Exact `gcp_project_id` value from `config.json`.\n\n## Outbox Relay Worker\n\nTriggered by Cloud Scheduler (1-minute).\n\nRequired variables:\n- `DATABASE_URL`: PostgreSQL connection string.\n- `PUBSUB_TOPIC`: Exact name of the main Pub/Sub topic.\n- `GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT`: Exact `gcp_project_id` value from `config.json`.\n\n## Audit Archiver Worker\n\nTriggered by Cloud Scheduler (5-minute).\n\nRequired variables:\n- `DATABASE_URL`: PostgreSQL connection string.\n- `GCS_BUCKET`: Exact name of the GCS audit bucket.\n- `GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT`: Exact `gcp_project_id` value from `config.json`.\n\n## Authentication & OAuth2 Token Exchange (`manifest.auth`)\n\nProvision an Identity Platform tenant (`google_identity_platform_tenant`) and three dedicated client IAM Service Accounts (`google_service_account`) with corresponding user-managed keys (`google_service_account_key`) for the `read` (`mediapulse/read`), `write` (`mediapulse/write`), and `admin` (`mediapulse/admin`) scopes.\n\n### How to Obtain and Output `client_id` and `client_secret`\nIn Terraform/OpenTofu, create:\n1. Three `google_service_account` resources (`client_read`, `client_write`, `client_admin`). Their `.email` attribute serves as both `<role>_client_email` and the OAuth2 `<role>_client_id`.\n2. Three `google_service_account_key` resources (`client_read`, `client_write`, `client_admin`) referencing each service account (`service_account_id = google_service_account.client_<role>.name`). Their `.private_key` attribute (base64-encoded key material generated by the GCP IAM service) serves as the OAuth2 `<role>_client_secret`.\n\nWrite these exact fields under `auth` in `/workspace/manifest.json`:\n- `tenant_id`: `google_identity_platform_tenant.<name>.name`\n- `resource_server_identifier`: `\"https://api.mediapulse.io\"` (or your API resource server URI)\n- `issuer`: `\"<gcp_endpoint_url>/identitytoolkit/v3/relyingparty/<gcp_project_id>\"`\n- `jwks_url`: `\"<gcp_endpoint_url>/robot/v1/metadata/jwk/securetoken@system.gserviceaccount.com\"` (pass this URL as `FIREBASE_AUTH_JWKS_URL` / `AUTH_JWKS_URL` to the Cloud Run API)\n- `token_endpoint`: `\"<gcp_endpoint_url>/oauth2/v4/token\"`\n- `read_client_email` and `read_client_id`: `google_service_account.client_read.email`\n- `write_client_email` and `write_client_id`: `google_service_account.client_write.email`\n- `admin_client_email` and `admin_client_id`: `google_service_account.client_admin.email`\n- `read_client_secret`: `google_service_account_key.client_read.private_key`\n- `write_client_secret`: `google_service_account_key.client_write.private_key`\n- `admin_client_secret`: `google_service_account_key.client_admin.private_key`\n\nTo obtain a Bearer JWT at runtime, send an HTTP `POST` (`application/x-www-form-urlencoded` or JSON) to `auth.token_endpoint` (`<gcp_endpoint_url>/oauth2/v4/token`) with `grant_type=client_credentials`, `client_id=<role_client_id>`, `client_secret=<role_client_secret>`, and `scope=mediapulse/<role>`.\n\n\n===INFRA===\n# Infrastructure Contract\n\nDeclare every required Google Cloud Platform (GCP) resource in Terraform or OpenTofu and maintain it in `infra/terraform.tfstate`.\n\n## Shared Rules\n\n- Configure `hashicorp/google` and `hashicorp/google-beta` with `gcp_project_id`, `region`, and custom endpoints pointing to `gcp_endpoint_url` (`http://gcp:4588`) from `/workspace/config/config.json`.\n- Prefix or label every managed resource with `resource_prefix` from `/workspace/config/config.json` and include the label `mediapulse_deployment=<resource_prefix>`.\n- Manage only resources belonging to this deployment; do not modify or delete pre-existing baseline resources.\n\n## Supported Floci-GCP Services Used\n\n| Service | Terraform Resource Family | Responsibility |\n|---|---|---|\n| **Compute Engine & VPC** | `google_compute_network`, `google_compute_subnetwork`, `google_compute_firewall` | Custom VPC, ingress and private subnets, and port-level ingress rules |\n| **Global External Load Balancer** | `google_compute_global_address`, `google_compute_region_network_endpoint_group`, `google_compute_backend_service`, `google_compute_url_map`, `google_compute_target_http_proxy`, `google_compute_global_forwarding_rule` | Public HTTP entry point routing via Serverless NEG to Cloud Run |\n| **Cloud Run (v2)** | `google_cloud_run_v2_service` | Runs the HTTP API container with `min_instance_count >= 2` |\n| **Cloud SQL for PostgreSQL** | `google_sql_database_instance`, `google_sql_database`, `google_sql_user` | Durable PostgreSQL 16 event store and transactional outbox |\n| **Secret Manager** | `google_secret_manager_secret`, `google_secret_manager_secret_version` | Stores and versions database credentials |\n| **Cloud Pub/Sub** | `google_pubsub_topic`, `google_pubsub_subscription` | Main event topic, push subscription to processor, and dead-letter topic/subscription |\n| **Cloud Tasks** | `google_cloud_tasks_queue` | Rate-limited queue for projection rebuild tasks |\n| **Cloud Functions (2nd gen)** | `google_cloudfunctions2_function` | Runs the Processor, Outbox Relay, and Audit Archiver workers |\n| **Cloud Firestore** | `google_firestore_database` | Native-mode document store for media read projections and version timelines |\n| **Cloud Datastore** | `google_firestore_index` or `google_datastore_index` | Low-latency entity cache (`collection = \"MediaCache\"` / kind) in front of Firestore |\n| **Cloud Storage (GCS) & BigQuery** | `google_storage_bucket`, `google_bigquery_dataset`, `google_bigquery_table` | Private versioned NDJSON audit archive and analytics dataset |\n| **Cloud Scheduler** | `google_cloud_scheduler_job` | Recurring OIDC cron triggers for the outbox relay (1m) and audit archiver (5m) |\n| **Identity Platform / Firebase Auth** | `google_identity_platform_tenant`, `google_service_account_key` | Scoped JWT authentication (`read`, `write`, `admin`) |\n| **Cloud IAM** | `google_service_account`, `google_project_iam_member`, `google_pubsub_topic_iam_member`, `google_pubsub_subscription_iam_member`, `google_storage_bucket_iam_member` | Per-component service accounts and least-privilege role bindings |\n| **Cloud KMS** | `google_kms_key_ring`, `google_kms_crypto_key` | KeyRing and 4 separate CMEK keys (`database`, `messaging`, `projection`, `audit`) |\n| **Cloud Logging & Monitoring** | `google_logging_project_bucket_config`, `google_logging_project_sink`, `google_monitoring_notification_channel` | 14-day structured log buckets and ops alerting channel |\n","metadata":{"output":"# Runtime\n\nThis file defines the required runtime behavior, service
